Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in biogeocenology theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 263 and the invariant I(A,B) = 265 mod 2.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any biogeocenology module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 98. The projective resolution has length at most 764 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 98 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the biogeocenology structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 764. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
